   240	    - [ ] Bubbles
   241	    - [ ] Verbose
   242	
   243	    Args:
   244	        mouse_position: The position of the mouse when released.
   245	    """
   246	
   247	    def __init__(self, mouse_position: Offset) -> None:
   248	        super().__init__()
   249	        self.mouse_position = mouse_position

   251

   254	
   255	
   256	class InputEvent(Event):
   257	    """Base class for input events."""
   258	
   259	
   260	@rich.repr.auto
   261	class Key(InputEvent):
   262	    """Sent when the user hits a key on the keyboard.
   263	
   264	    - [X] Bubbles
   265	    - [ ] Verbose
   266	
   267	    Args:
   268	        key: The key that was pressed.
   269	        character: A printable character or `None` if it is not printable.
   270	    """
   271	
   272	    __slots__ = ["key", "character", "aliases"]
   273	
   274	    def __init__(self, key: str, character: str | None) -> None:
   275	        super().__init__()
   276	        self.key = key
   277	        """The key that was pressed."""
   278	        self.character = (
   279	            (key if len(key) == 1 else None) if character is None else character
   280	        )
   281	        """A printable character or ``None`` if it is not printable."""
   282	        self.aliases: list[str] = _get_key_aliases(key)
   283	        """The aliases for the key, including the key itself."""
   284	
   285	    def __rich_repr__(self) -> rich.repr.Result:
   286	        yield "key", self.key
   287	        yield "character", self.character
   288	        yield "name", self.name
   289	        yield "is_printable", self.is_printable
   290	        yield "aliases", self.aliases, [self.key]
   291	
   292	    @property
   293	    def name(self) -> str:
   294	        """Name of a key suitable for use as a Python identifier."""
   295	        return _key_to_identifier(self.key).lower()
   296	
   297	    @property
   298	    def name_aliases(self) -> list[str]:
   299	        """The corresponding name for every alias in `aliases` list."""
   300	        return [_key_to_identifier(key) for key in self.aliases]
   301	
   302	    @property
   303	    def is_printable(self) -> bool:
   304	        """Check if the key is printable (produces a unicode character).
   305	
   306	        Returns:
   307	            `True` if the key is printable.
   308	        """
   309	        return False if self.character is None else self.character.isprintable()
   310	
   311	
   312	def _key_to_identifier(key: str) -> str:
   313	    """Convert the key string to a name suitable for use as a Python identifier."""
   314	    key_no_modifiers = key.split("+")[-1]
   315	    if len(key_no_modifiers) == 1 and key_no_modifiers.isupper():
   316	        if "+" in key:
   317	            key = f"{key.rpartition('+')[0]}+upper_{key_no_modifiers}"
   318	        else:
   319	            key = f"upper_{key_no_modifiers}"
   320	    return key.replace("+", "_").lower()
   321	
   322	
   323	@rich.repr.auto
   324	class MouseEvent(InputEvent, bubble=True):
   325	    """Sent in response to a mouse event.
   326	
   327	    - [X] Bubbles
   328	    - [ ] Verbose
   329	
   330	    Args:
   331	        widget: The widget under the mouse.
   332	        x: The relative x coordinate.
   333	        y: The relative y coordinate.
   334	        delta_x: Change in x since the last message.
   335	        delta_y: Change in y since the last message.
   336	        button: Indexed of the pressed button.
   337	        shift: True if the shift key is pressed.
   338	        meta: True if the meta key is pressed.
   339	        ctrl: True if the ctrl key is pressed.
   340	        screen_x: The absolute x coordinate.
   341	        screen_y: The absolute y coordinate.
   342	        style: The Rich Style under the mouse cursor.
   343	    """
   344	
   345	    __slots__ = [
   346	        "widget",
   347	        "_x",
   348	        "_y",
   349	        "_delta_x",
   350	        "_delta_y",
   351	        "button",
   352	        "shift",
   353	        "meta",
   354	        "ctrl",
   355	        "_screen_x",
   356	        "_screen_y",
   357	        "_style",
   358	    ]
   359	
   360	    def __init__(
   361	        self,
   362	        widget: Widget | None,
   363	        x: float,
   364	        y: float,
   365	        delta_x: int,
   366	        delta_y: int,
   367	        button: int,
   368	        shift: bool,
   369	        meta: bool,
   370	        ctrl: bool,
   371	        screen_x: float | None = None,
   372	        screen_y: float | None = None,
   373	        style: Style | None = None,
   374	    ) -> None:
   375	        super().__init__()
   376	        self.widget: Widget | None = widget
   377	        """The widget under the mouse at the time of a click."""
   378	        self._x = x
   379	        """The relative x coordinate."""
   380	        self._y = y
   381	        """The relative y coordinate."""
   382	        self._delta_x = delta_x
   383	        """Change in x since the last message."""
   384	        self._delta_y = delta_y
   385	        """Change in y since the last message."""
   386	        self.button = button
   387	        """Indexed of the pressed button."""
   388	        self.shift = shift
   389	        """`True` if the shift key is pressed."""
   390	        self.meta = meta
   391	        """`True` if the meta key is pressed."""
   392	        self.ctrl = ctrl
   393	        """`True` if the ctrl key is pressed."""
   394	        self._screen_x = x if screen_x is None else screen_x
   395	        """The absolute x coordinate."""
   396	        self._screen_y = y if screen_y is None else screen_y
   397	        """The absolute y coordinate."""
   398	        self._style = style or Style()
   399	
   400	    @property
   401	    def x(self) -> int:
   402	        """The relative X coordinate of the cell under the mouse."""
   403	        return int(self._x)
   404	
   405	    @property
   406	    def y(self) -> int:
   407	        """The relative Y coordinate of the cell under the mouse."""
   408	        return int(self._y)
   409	
   410	    @property
   411	    def delta_x(self) -> int:
   412	        """Change in `x` since last message."""
   413	        return self._delta_x
   414	
   415	    @property
   416	    def delta_y(self) -> int:
   417	        """Change in `y` since the last message."""
   418	        return self._delta_y
   419	
   420	    @property
   421	    def screen_x(self) -> int:
   422	        """X coordinate of the cell relative to top left of screen."""
   423	        return int(self._screen_x)
   424	
   425	    @property
   426	    def screen_y(self) -> int:
   427	        """Y coordinate of the cell relative to top left of screen."""
   428	        return int(self._screen_y)
   429	
   430	    @property

    74	    def parse_mouse_code(self, code: str) -> Message | None:
    75	        sgr_match = self._re_sgr_mouse.match(code)
    76	        if sgr_match:

    83	                return None

    88	            ):

    90	                width, height = self.terminal_size
    91	                x_ratio = pixel_width / width
    92	                y_ratio = pixel_height / height
    93	                x /= x_ratio
    94	                y /= y_ratio
    95	
    96	            delta_x = int(x) - int(self.last_x)
    97	            delta_y = int(y) - int(self.last_y)

   166	        def reissue_sequence_as_keys(
   167	            reissue_sequence: str, process_alt: bool = False
   168	        ) -> None:
   169	            """Called when an escape sequence hasn't been understood.
   170	
   171	            Args:
   172	                reissue_sequence: Key sequence to report to the app.
   173	            """
   174	
   175	            alt = False
   176	
   177	            if reissue_sequence:
   178	                self.debug_log("REISSUE", repr(reissue_sequence))
   179	                for character in reissue_sequence:
   180	                    if process_alt and character == ESC:
   181	                        alt = True
   182	                        continue
   183	                    key_events = sequence_to_key_events(character, alt=alt)
   184	                    for event in key_events:
   185	                        if event.key == "escape" and not process_alt:
   186	                            event = events.Key("circumflex_accent", "^")
   187	                        on_token(event)
   188	                    alt = False
   189	
   190	        while not self.is_eof:
   191	            if not bracketed_paste and paste_buffer:
   192	                # We're at the end of the bracketed paste.
   193	                # The paste buffer has content, but the bracketed paste has finished,
   194	                # so we flush the paste buffer. We have to remove the final character
   195	                # since if bracketed paste has come to an end, we'll have added the
   196	                # ESC from the closing bracket, since at that point we didn't know what
   197	                # the full escape code was.
   198	                pasted_text = "".join(paste_buffer[:-1])
   199	                # Note the removal of NUL characters: https://github.com/Textualize/textual/issues/1661
   200	                on_token(events.Paste(pasted_text.replace("\x00", "")))
   201	                paste_buffer.clear()
   202	
   203	            try:
   204	                character = yield read1()
   205	            except ParseEOF:
   206	                return
   207	
   208	            if bracketed_paste:
   209	                paste_buffer.append(character)
   210	
   211	            self.debug_log(f"character={character!r}")
   212	            if character != ESC:
   213	                if not bracketed_paste:
   214	                    for event in sequence_to_key_events(character):
   215	                        on_key_token(event)
   216	                if not character:
   217	                    return
   218	                continue
   219	
   220	            # # Could be the escape key was pressed OR the start of an escape sequence
   221	            sequence: str = ESC
   222	
   223	            def send_sequence(process_alt: bool = True) -> None:
   224	                """Send escape key and reissue sequence."""
   225	                if sequence == ESC:
   226	                    on_token(events.Key("escape", "\x1b"))
   227	                else:
   228	                    reissue_sequence_as_keys(sequence, process_alt=process_alt)
   229	
   230	            while True:
   231	                try:
   232	                    new_character = yield read1(constants.ESCAPE_DELAY)
   233	                except ParseTimeout:
   234	                    send_sequence()
   235	                    break
   236	                except ParseEOF:
   237	                    send_sequence()
   238	                    return
   239	
   240	                if new_character == ESC:
   241	                    send_sequence(process_alt=False)
   242	                    sequence = character
   243	                    continue
   244	                else:
   245	                    sequence += new_character
   246	                    if len(sequence) > _MAX_SEQUENCE_SEARCH_THRESHOLD:
   247	                        reissue_sequence_as_keys(sequence)
   248	                        break
   249	
   250	                self.debug_log(f"sequence={sequence!r}")
   251	                if sequence in SPECIAL_SEQUENCES:
   252	                    if sequence == FOCUSIN:
   253	                        on_token(events.AppFocus())
   254	                    elif sequence == FOCUSOUT:
   255	                        on_token(events.AppBlur())
   256	                    elif sequence == BRACKETED_PASTE_START:
   257	                        bracketed_paste = True
   258	                    elif sequence == BRACKETED_PASTE_END:
   259	                        bracketed_paste = False
   260	                    break

   327	    def _sequence_to_key_events(
   328	        self, sequence: str, alt: bool = False
   329	    ) -> Iterable[events.Key]:
   330	        """Map a sequence of code points on to a sequence of keys.
   331	
   332	        Args:
   333	            sequence: Sequence of code points.
   334	
   335	        Returns:
   336	            Keys
   337	        """
   338	
   339	        if (match := _re_extended_key.fullmatch(sequence)) is not None:
   340	            number, modifiers, end = match.groups()
   341	            number = number or 1
   342	            if not (key := FUNCTIONAL_KEYS.get(f"{number}{end}", "")):
   343	                try:
   344	                    key = _character_to_key(chr(int(number)))
   345	                except Exception:
   346	                    key = chr(int(number))
   347	            key_tokens: list[str] = []
   348	            if modifiers:
   349	                modifier_bits = int(modifiers) - 1
   350	                # Not convinced of the utility in reporting caps_lock and num_lock
   351	                MODIFIERS = ("shift", "alt", "ctrl", "super", "hyper", "meta")
   352	                # Ignore caps_lock and num_lock modifiers
   353	                for bit, modifier in enumerate(MODIFIERS):
   354	                    if modifier_bits & (1 << bit):
   355	                        key_tokens.append(modifier)
   356	
   357	            key_tokens.sort()
   358	            key_tokens.append(key.lower())
   359	            yield events.Key(
   360	                "+".join(key_tokens), sequence if len(sequence) == 1 else None
   361	            )
   362	            return
   363	
   364	        keys = ANSI_SEQUENCES_KEYS.get(sequence)
   365	        # If we're being asked to ignore the key...
   366	        if keys is IGNORE_SEQUENCE:
   367	            # ...build a special ignore key event, which has the ignore
   368	            # name as the key (that is, the key this sequence is bound
   369	            # to is the ignore key) and the sequence that was ignored as
   370	            # the character.
   371	            yield events.Key(Keys.Ignore, sequence)
   372	            return
   373	        if isinstance(keys, tuple):
   374	            # If the sequence mapped to a tuple, then it's values from the
   375	            # `Keys` enum. Raise key events from what we find in the tuple.
   376	            for key in keys:
   377	                yield events.Key(key.value, sequence if len(sequence) == 1 else None)
   378	            return
   379	        # If keys is a string, the intention is that it's a mapping to a
   380	        # character, which should really be treated as the sequence for the
   381	        # purposes of the next step...
   382	        if isinstance(keys, str):
   383	            sequence = keys
   384	        # If the sequence is a single character, attempt to process it as a
   385	        # key.
   386	        if len(sequence) == 1:
   387	            try:
   388	                if not sequence.isalnum():
   389	                    name = _character_to_key(sequence)
   390	                else:
   391	                    name = sequence
   392	
   393	                name = KEY_NAME_REPLACEMENTS.get(name, name)
   394	                if len(name) == 1 and alt:
   395	                    if name.isupper():
   396	                        name = f"shift+{name.lower()}"
   397	                    name = f"alt+{name}"
   398	                yield events.Key(name, sequence)
   399	            except Exception:
   400	                yield events.Key(sequence, sequence)

   363	
   364	        keys = ANSI_SEQUENCES_KEYS.get(sequence)
   365	        # If we're being asked to ignore the key...
   366	        if keys is IGNORE_SEQUENCE:
   367	            # ...build a special ignore key event, which has the ignore
   368	            # name as the key (that is, the key this sequence is bound
   369	            # to is the ignore key) and the sequence that was ignored as
   370	            # the character.
   371	            yield events.Key(Keys.Ignore, sequence)
   372	            return
   373	        if isinstance(keys, tuple):
   374	            # If the sequence mapped to a tuple, then it's values from the
   375	            # `Keys` enum. Raise key events from what we find in the tuple.
   376	            for key in keys:
   377	                yield events.Key(key.value, sequence if len(sequence) == 1 else None)
   378	            return
   379	        # If keys is a string, the intention is that it's a mapping to a
   380	        # character, which should really be treated as the sequence for the
   381	        # purposes of the next step...
   382	        if isinstance(keys, str):
   383	            sequence = keys
   384	        # If the sequence is a single character, attempt to process it as a
   385	        # key.
   386	        if len(sequence) == 1:
   387	            try:
   388	                if not sequence.isalnum():
   389	                    name = _character_to_key(sequence)
   390	                else:
   391	                    name = sequence
   392	
   393	                name = KEY_NAME_REPLACEMENTS.get(name, name)
   394	                if len(name) == 1 and alt:
   395	                    if name.isupper():
   396	                        name = f"shift+{name.lower()}"
   397	                    name = f"alt+{name}"
   398	                yield events.Key(name, sequence)
   399	            except Exception:
   400	                yield events.Key(sequence, sequence)

END STEP

    19	
    20	def chunks(data, size):
    21	    if size == 0:
    22	        yield data
    23	        return
    24	
    25	    chunk_start = 0
    26	    chunk_end = size
    27	    while True:
    28	        yield data[chunk_start:chunk_end]
    29	        chunk_start = chunk_end
    30	        chunk_end += size
    31	        if chunk_end >= len(data):
    32	            yield data[chunk_start:chunk_end]
    33	            break
    34	
    35	
    36	@pytest.fixture
    37	def parser():
    38	    return XTermParser()
    39

    41	@pytest.mark.parametrize("chunk_size", [2, 3, 4, 5, 6])
    42	def test_varying_parser_chunk_sizes_no_missing_data(parser, chunk_size):
    43	    end = "\x1b[8~"
    44	    text = "ABCDEFGH"
    45	
    46	    data = end + text
    47	    events = []
    48	    for chunk in chunks(data, chunk_size):
    49	        events.append(parser.feed(chunk))
    50	
    51	    events = list(itertools.chain.from_iterable(list(event) for event in events))
    52	
    53	    assert events[0].key == "end"
    54	    assert [event.key for event in events[1:]] == list(text)
    55

    57	def test_bracketed_paste(parser):
    58	    """When bracketed paste mode is enabled in the terminal emulator and
    59	    the user pastes in some text, it will surround the pasted input
    60	    with the escape codes "\x1b[200~" and "\x1b[201~". The text between
    61	    these codes corresponds to a single `Paste` event in Textual.
    62	    """
    63	    pasted_text = "PASTED"
    64	    events = list(parser.feed(f"\x1b[200~{pasted_text}\x1b[201~"))
    65	
    66	    assert len(events) == 1
    67	    assert isinstance(events[0], Paste)
    68	    assert events[0].text == pasted_text
    69	
    70	
    71	def test_bracketed_paste_content_contains_escape_codes(parser):
    72	    """When performing a bracketed paste, if the pasted content contains
    73	    supported ANSI escape sequences, it should not interfere with the paste,
    74	    and no escape sequences within the bracketed paste should be converted
    75	    into Textual events.
    76	    """
    77	    pasted_text = "PAS\x0fTED"
    78	    events = list(parser.feed(f"\x1b[200~{pasted_text}\x1b[201~"))
    79	    assert len(events) == 1
    80	    assert events[0].text == pasted_text

    83	def test_bracketed_paste_amongst_other_codes(parser):
    84	    pasted_text = "PASTED"
    85	    events = list(parser.feed(f"\x1b[8~\x1b[200~{pasted_text}\x1b[201~\x1b[8~"))
    86	    assert len(events) == 3  # Key.End -> Paste -> Key.End
    87	    assert events[0].key == "end"
    88	    assert events[1].text == pasted_text
    89	    assert events[2].key == "end"

    92	def test_cant_match_escape_sequence_too_long(parser):
    93	    """The sequence did not match, and we hit the maximum sequence search
    94	    length threshold, so each character should be issued as a key-press instead.
    95	    """
    96	    sequence = "\x1b[123456789123456789123123456789123456789123"
    97	    events = list(parser.feed(sequence))
    98	
    99	    # Every character in the sequence is converted to a key press
   100	    assert len(events) == len(sequence)
   101	    assert all(isinstance(event, Key) for event in events)
   102	
   103	    # When we backtrack '\x1b' is translated to '^'
   104	    assert events[0].key == "circumflex_accent"
   105	
   106	    # The rest of the characters correspond to the expected key presses
   107	    events = events[1:]
   108	    for index, character in enumerate(sequence[1:]):
   109	        assert events[index].character == character

    27	async def test_character_bindings():
    28	    """Test you can bind to a character as well as a longer key name."""
    29	    counter = 0
    30	
    31	    class BindApp(App):
    32	        BINDINGS = [(".,~,space", "increment", "foo")]
    33	
    34	        def action_increment(self) -> None:
    35	            nonlocal counter
    36	            counter += 1
    37

    44	        await pilot.pause()
    45	        assert counter == 2
    46	        await pilot.press(" ")
    47	        await pilot.pause()
    48	        assert counter == 3
    49	        await pilot.press("x")
    50	        await pilot.pause()
    51	        assert counter == 3

   203	# Unicode db contains some obscure names
   204	# This mapping replaces them with more common terms
   205	KEY_NAME_REPLACEMENTS = {
   206	    "solidus": "slash",
   207	    "reverse_solidus": "backslash",
   208	    "commercial_at": "at",
   209	    "hyphen_minus": "minus",

   215	# Convert the friendly versions of character key Unicode names
   216	# back to their original names.
   217	# This is because we go from Unicode to friendly by replacing spaces and dashes
   218	# with underscores, which cannot be undone by replacing underscores with spaces/dashes.
   219	KEY_TO_UNICODE_NAME = {

   246	# Some keys have aliases. For example, if you press `ctrl+m` on your keyboard,
   247	# it's treated the same way as if you press `enter`. Key handlers `key_ctrl_m` and
   248	# `key_enter` are both valid in this case.
   249	KEY_ALIASES = {
   250	    "tab": ["ctrl+i"],
   251	    "enter": ["ctrl+m"],
   252	    "escape": ["ctrl+left_square_brace"],
   253	    "ctrl+at": ["ctrl+space"],

   276	def _get_unicode_name_from_key(key: str) -> str:
   277	    """Get the best guess for the Unicode name of the char corresponding to the key.
   278	
   279	    This function can be seen as a pseudo-inverse of the function `_character_to_key`.
   280	    """
   281	    return KEY_TO_UNICODE_NAME.get(key, key)
   282	
   283	
   284	def _get_key_aliases(key: str) -> list[str]:
   285	    """Return all aliases for the given key, including the key itself"""
   286	    return [key] + KEY_ALIASES.get(key, [])
   287	
   288	
   289	@lru_cache(1024)
   290	def format_key(key: str) -> str:
   291	    """Given a key (i.e. the `key` string argument to Binding __init__),
   292	    return the value that should be displayed in the app when referring
   293	    to this key (e.g. in the Footer widget)."""
   294	
   295	    display_alias = KEY_DISPLAY_ALIASES.get(key)
   296	    if display_alias:
   297	        return display_alias
   298	
   299	    original_key = REPLACED_KEYS.get(key, key)
   300	    tentative_unicode_name = _get_unicode_name_from_key(original_key)
   301	    try:
   302	        unicode_name = unicodedata.lookup(tentative_unicode_name)
   303	    except KeyError:
   304	        pass
   305	    else:
   306	        if unicode_name.isprintable():
   307	            return unicode_name
   308	    return tentative_unicode_name
   309	
   310	
   311	@lru_cache(1024)
   312	def key_to_character(key: str) -> str | None:
   313	    """Given a key identifier, return the character associated with it.
   314	
   315	    Args:
   316	        key: The key identifier.
   317	
   318	    Returns:
   319	        A key if one could be found, otherwise `None`.
   320	    """
   321	    _, separator, key = key.rpartition("+")
   322	    if separator:
   323	        # If there is a separator, then it means a modifier (other than shift) is applied.
   324	        # Keys with modifiers, don't come from printable keys.
   325	        return None
   326	    if len(key) == 1:
   327	        # Key identifiers with a length of one, are also characters.
   328	        return key
   329	    try:
   330	        return unicodedata.lookup(KEY_TO_UNICODE_NAME[key])
   331	    except KeyError:
   332	        pass
   333	    try:
   334	        return unicodedata.lookup(key.replace("_", " ").upper())
   335	    except KeyError:
   336	        pass
   337	    # Return None if we couldn't identify the key.
   338	    return None
   339	
   340	
   341	def _character_to_key(character: str) -> str:
   342	    """Convert a single character to a key value.
   343	
   344	    This transformation can be undone by the function `_get_unicode_name_from_key`.
   345	    """
   346	    if not character.isalnum():
   347	        try:
   348	            key = (
   349	                unicodedata.name(character).lower().replace("-", "_").replace(" ", "_")
   350	            )
   351	        except ValueError:
   352	            key = ASCII_KEY_NAMES.get(character, character)
   353	    else:
   354	        key = character
   355	    key = KEY_NAME_REPLACEMENTS.get(key, key)
   356	    return key
   357	
   358	
   359	def _normalize_key_list(keys: str) -> str:
   360	    """Normalizes a comma separated list of keys.
   361	
   362	    Replaces single letter keys with full name.
   363	    """
   364	
   365	    keys_list = [key.strip() for key in keys.split(",")]
   366	    return ",".join(
   367	        _character_to_key(key) if len(key) == 1 else key for key in keys_list
   368	    )

END STEP

## [6.6.0] - 2025-11-10

### Fixed

- Fixed `TextArea` cursor display on wrapped lines https://github.com/Textualize/textual/pull/6196
- Fixed `remove_children` not refreshing layout https://github.com/Textualize/textual/pull/6206
- Fixed flicker with :hover pseudo class https://github.com/Textualize/textual/pull/6214
- Fixed scrollbar not updating after textarea paste https://github.com/Textualize/textual/pull/6219

### Added

- Added `grid_size` property to `GridLayout` https://github.com/Textualize/textual/pull/6210
- Exposed `NoSelection` and `BLANK` via `textual.widgets.select` https://github.com/Textualize/textual/pull/6214
- Added `Widget.FOCUS_ON_CLICK` classvar amd `Widget.focus_on_click` method https://github.com/Textualize/textual/pull/6216
- Added support for the kitty keyboard protocol on Windows https://github.com/Textualize/textual/pull/6207
- Added `Widget.mount_compose` https://github.com/Textualize/textual/pull/6216

### Changed

- Change highlight style of Select to only highlight the border, not the label https://github.com/Textualize/textual/pull/6214

## [6.5.0] - 2025-10-31

### Added

## [0.67.1] - 2024-06-12

### Changed

- Reverts Vim keys in DataTable, provides alternatives https://github.com/Textualize/textual/pull/4638

## [0.67.0] - 2024-06-11

### Added

- Added support for Kitty's key protocol https://github.com/Textualize/textual/pull/4631
- `ctrl+pageup`/`ctrl+pagedown` will scroll page left/right in DataTable https://github.com/Textualize/textual/pull/4633
- `g`/`G` will scroll to the top/bottom of the DataTable https://github.com/Textualize/textual/pull/4633
- Added simple `hjkl` key bindings to move the cursor in DataTable https://github.com/Textualize/textual/pull/4633

### Changed

- `home` and `end` now works horizontally instead of vertically in DataTable https://github.com/Textualize/textual/pull/4633
- `Tree` and `DirectoryTree` nodes now have a bigger click target, spanning the full line https://github.com/Textualize/textual/pull/4636

### Fixed

- Fixed pageup/pagedown behavior in DataTable https://github.com/Textualize/textual/pull/4633
- Added `App.CLOSE_TIMEOUT` https://github.com/Textualize/textual/pull/4635
- Fixed deadlock on shutdown https://github.com/Textualize/textual/pull/4635

from textual._xterm_parser import XTermParser

def parse(seq):
    p = XTermParser()
    evs = list(p.feed(seq))
    evs.extend(p.feed(""))
    return evs

for seq in ["\x1b[97;3u", "\x1b[65;4u", "\x1b[120;7u", "\x1b[97;1;1u", "\x1b[97;3:2u", "\x1b[97;3:3u", "\x1b[13;3u", "\x1b[32;3u", "\x1b[0;3u"]:
    evs = parse(seq)
    print(repr(seq))
    for e in evs:
        print("   key=%r char=%r aliases=%r repr=%s" % (e.key, e.character, e.aliases, e))
    print()

def parse(seq):
    p = XTermParser()

    evs.extend(p.feed(""))
    return evs

for seq in ["\x1b ", "\x1b\r", "\x1b\x7f", "\x1ba", "\x1bA", "\x1b\x01", "\x1b\t", "\x1b\n", "\x1b[13~", "\x1b[127u"]:

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Mapping, Tuple

from typing_extensions import Final

from textual.keys import Keys


class IgnoredSequence:
    """Class used to mark that a sequence should be ignored."""


IGNORE_SEQUENCE: Final[IgnoredSequence] = IgnoredSequence()
"""Constant to indicate that a sequence should be ignored."""


# Mapping of vt100 escape codes to Keys.
ANSI_SEQUENCES_KEYS: Mapping[str, Tuple[Keys, ...] | str | IgnoredSequence] = {
    # Control keys.
    " ": (Keys.Space,),
    "\r": (Keys.Enter,),
    "\x00": (Keys.ControlAt,),  # Control-At (Also for Ctrl-Space)
    "\x01": (Keys.ControlA,),  # Control-A (home)
    "\x02": (Keys.ControlB,),  # Control-B (emacs cursor left)
    "\x03": (Keys.ControlC,),  # Control-C (interrupt)
    "\x04": (Keys.ControlD,),  # Control-D (exit)
    "\x05": (Keys.ControlE,),  # Control-E (end)
    "\x06": (Keys.ControlF,),  # Control-F (cursor forward)
    "\x07": (Keys.ControlG,),  # Control-G
    "\x08": (Keys.Backspace,),  # Control-H (8) (Identical to '\b')
    "\x09": (Keys.Tab,),  # Control-I (9) (Identical to '\t')
    "\x0a": (Keys.ControlJ,),  # Control-J (10) (Identical to '\n')
    "\x0b": (Keys.ControlK,),  # Control-K (delete until end of line; vertical tab)
    "\x0c": (Keys.ControlL,),  # Control-L (clear; form feed)
    # "\x0d": (Keys.ControlM,),  # Control-M (13) (Identical to '\r')
    "\x0e": (Keys.ControlN,),  # Control-N (14) (history forward)
    "\x0f": (Keys.ControlO,),  # Control-O (15)
    "\x10": (Keys.ControlP,),  # Control-P (16) (history back)
    "\x11": (Keys.ControlQ,),  # Control-Q
    "\x12": (Keys.ControlR,),  # Control-R (18) (reverse search)
    "\x13": (Keys.ControlS,),  # Control-S (19) (forward search)
    "\x14": (Keys.ControlT,),  # Control-T
    "\x15": (Keys.ControlU,),  # Control-U
    "\x16": (Keys.ControlV,),  # Control-V
    "\x17": (Keys.ControlW,),  # Control-W

    "\x19": (Keys.ControlY,),  # Control-Y (25)

    "\x1b": (Keys.Escape,),  # Also Control-[

===== events Key =====
261:class Key(InputEvent):
class Key(InputEvent):
    """Sent when the user hits a key on the keyboard.

    - [X] Bubbles
    - [ ] Verbose

    Args:
        key: The key that was pressed.
        character: A printable character or `None` if it is not printable.
    """

    __slots__ = ["key", "character"]

    def __init__(self, key: str, character: str | None) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.key = key
        """The key that was pressed."""
        self.character = (
            (key if len(key) == 1 else None) if character is None else character
        )
        """A printable character or ``None`` if it is not printable."""

    def __rich_repr__(self) -> rich.repr.Result:
        yield "key", self.key
        yield "character", self.character
        yield "name", self.name
        yield "is_printable", self.is_printable
        yield "aliases", self.aliases, [self.key]

    def copy(self) -> Key:
        """Get a copy of this key event."""
        return Key(self.key, self.character)

    @property
    def name(self) -> str:
        """Name of a key suitable for use as a Python identifier."""
        return _key_to_identifier(self.key).lower()

    @property
    def name_aliases(self) -> list[str]:
        """The corresponding name for every alias in `aliases` list."""
        return [_key_to_identifier(key) for key in self.aliases]

    @property
    def is_printable(self) -> bool:
        """Check if the key is printable (produces a unicode character).

        Returns:
            `True` if the key is printable.
        """
        return False if self.character is None else self.character.isprintable()

    @property
    def aliases(self) -> list[str]:
        """The aliases for the key, including the key itself."""
        return _get_key_aliases(self.key)


def _key_to_identifier(key: str) -> str:
    """Convert the key string to a name suitable for use as a Python identifier."""
    key_no_modifiers = key.split("+")[-1]
    if len(key_no_modifiers) == 1 and key_no_modifiers.isupper():
        if "+" in key:
            key = f"{key.rpartition('+')[0]}+upper_{key_no_modifiers}"
        else:
            key = f"upper_{key_no_modifiers}"
    return key.replace("+", "_").lower()

    - [X] Bubbles
    - [ ] Verbose

    Args:
        widget: The widget under the mouse.
        x: The relative x coordinate.
        y: The relative y coordinate.
        delta_x: Change in x since the last message.

class XTermParser(Parser[Message]):
    _re_sgr_mouse = re.compile(r"\x1b\[<(\d+);(-?\d+);(-?\d+)([Mm])")

    def __init__(self, debug: bool = False) -> None:
        self.last_x = 0.0
        self.last_y = 0.0
        self.mouse_pixels = False
        self.terminal_size: tuple[int, int] | None = None
        self.terminal_pixel_size: tuple[int, int] | None = None
        self._debug_log_file = open("keys.log", "at") if debug else None
        super().__init__()
        self.debug_log("---")

    def debug_log(self, *args: Any) -> None:  # pragma: no cover
        if self._debug_log_file is not None:
            self._debug_log_file.write(" ".join(args) + "\n")
            self._debug_log_file.flush()

    def feed(self, data: str) -> Iterable[Message]:
        self.debug_log(f"FEED {data!r}")
        return super().feed(data)

    def parse_mouse_code(self, code: str) -> Message | None:
        sgr_match = self._re_sgr_mouse.match(code)
        if sgr_match:
            _buttons, _x, _y, state = sgr_match.groups()
            buttons = int(_buttons)
            x = float(int(_x) - 1)
            y = float(int(_y) - 1)
            if x < 0 or y < 0:
                # TODO: Workaround for Ghostty erroneous negative coordinate bug
                return None
            if (
                self.mouse_pixels
                and self.terminal_pixel_size is not None
                and self.terminal_size is not None
            ):
                pixel_width, pixel_height = self.terminal_pixel_size
                width, height = self.terminal_size
                x_ratio = pixel_width / width
                y_ratio = pixel_height / height
                x /= x_ratio
                y /= y_ratio

            delta_x = int(x) - int(self.last_x)
            delta_y = int(y) - int(self.last_y)
            self.last_x = x
            self.last_y = y
            event_class: type[events.MouseEvent]

            if buttons & 64:
                event_class = [
                    events.MouseScrollUp,
                    events.MouseScrollDown,
                    events.MouseScrollLeft,
                    events.MouseScrollRight,
                ][buttons & 3]
                button = 0
            else:
=== sequence_to_key_events ===

            if isinstance(token, events.Resize):
                self.terminal_size = token.size
                self.terminal_pixel_size = token.pixel_size
            token_callback(token)

        def on_key_token(event: events.Key) -> None:
            """Token callback wrapper for handling keys.

            Args:
                event: The key event to send to the callback.

            This wrapper looks for keys that should be ignored, and filters
            them out, logging the ignored sequence when it does.
            """
            if event.key == Keys.Ignore:
                self.debug_log(f"ignored={event.character!r}")
            else:
                on_token(event)

        def reissue_sequence_as_keys(
            reissue_sequence: str, process_alt: bool = False
        ) -> None:
            """Called when an escape sequence hasn't been understood.

            Args:
                reissue_sequence: Key sequence to report to the app.
            """

            alt = False

            if reissue_sequence:
                self.debug_log("REISSUE", repr(reissue_sequence))
                for character in reissue_sequence:
                    if process_alt and character == ESC:
                        alt = True
                        continue
                    key_events = sequence_to_key_events(character, alt=alt)
                    for event in key_events:
                        if event.key == "escape" and not process_alt:
                            event = events.Key("circumflex_accent", "^")
                        on_token(event)
                    alt = False

        while not self.is_eof:
            if not bracketed_paste and paste_buffer:
                # We're at the end of the bracketed paste.
                # The paste buffer has content, but the bracketed paste has finished,
                # so we flush the paste buffer. We have to remove the final character
                # since if bracketed paste has come to an end, we'll have added the
                # ESC from the closing bracket, since at that point we didn't know what
                # the full escape code was.
                pasted_text = "".join(paste_buffer[:-1])
                # Note the removal of NUL characters: https://github.com/Textualize/textual/issues/1661
                on_token(events.Paste(pasted_text.replace("\x00", "")))
                paste_buffer.clear()

            try:
                character = yield read1()
            except ParseEOF:
                return

            if bracketed_paste:
                paste_buffer.append(character)

            self.debug_log(f"character={character!r}")
            if character != ESC:
                if not bracketed_paste:
                    for event in sequence_to_key_events(character):
                        on_key_token(event)
                if not character:
                    return
                continue

            # # Could be the escape key was pressed OR the start of an escape sequence
            sequence: str = ESC

            def send_sequence(process_alt: bool = True) -> None:
                """Send escape key and reissue sequence."""
                if sequence == ESC:
                    on_token(events.Key("escape", "\x1b"))
                else:
                    reissue_sequence_as_keys(sequence, process_alt=process_alt)

            while True:
                try:
                    new_character = yield read1(constants.ESCAPE_DELAY)
                except ParseTimeout:
                    send_sequence()
                    break
                except ParseEOF:
                    send_sequence()
                    return

                if new_character == ESC:
                    send_sequence(process_alt=False)
                    sequence = character
                    continue
                else:
                    sequence += new_character
                    if len(sequence) > _MAX_SEQUENCE_SEARCH_THRESHOLD:
                        reissue_sequence_as_keys(sequence)
                        break

                self.debug_log(f"sequence={sequence!r}")
                if sequence in SPECIAL_SEQUENCES:
                    if sequence == FOCUSIN:
                        on_token(events.AppFocus())
                    elif sequence == FOCUSOUT:
                        on_token(events.AppBlur())
                    elif sequence == BRACKETED_PASTE_START:
                        bracketed_paste = True
                    elif sequence == BRACKETED_PASTE_END:
                        bracketed_paste = False
                    break
                if match := _re_in_band_window_resize.fullmatch(sequence):
                    height, width, pixel_height, pixel_width = [
                        group.partition(":")[0] for group in match.groups()
                    ]
                    resize_event = events.Resize.from_dimensions(
                        (int(width), int(height)),
                        (int(pixel_width), int(pixel_height)),
                    )

                    self.terminal_size = resize_event.size
                    self.terminal_pixel_size = resize_event.pixel_size
                    self.mouse_pixels = True
                    on_token(resize_event)
                    break

                if not bracketed_paste:
                    # Check cursor position report
                    cursor_position_match = _re_cursor_position.match(sequence)
                    if cursor_position_match is not None:
                        row, column = map(int, cursor_position_match.groups())

END STEP

    def _sequence_to_key_events(
        self, sequence: str, alt: bool = False
    ) -> Iterable[events.Key]:
        """Map a sequence of code points on to a sequence of keys.

        Args:
            sequence: Sequence of code points.

        Returns:
            Iterable of key events.
        """

        if (
            not constants.DISABLE_KITTY_KEY
            and (keys := self._parse_extended_key(sequence)) is not None
        ):
            for key in keys:
                yield key.copy()
            return

        keys = ANSI_SEQUENCES_KEYS.get(sequence)
        # If we're being asked to ignore the key...
        if keys is IGNORE_SEQUENCE:
            # ...build a special ignore key event, which has the ignore
            # name as the key (that is, the key this sequence is bound
            # to is the ignore key) and the sequence that was ignored as
            # the character.
            yield events.Key(Keys.Ignore, sequence)
            return
        if isinstance(keys, tuple):
            # If the sequence mapped to a tuple, then it's values from the
            # `Keys` enum. Raise key events from what we find in the tuple.
            for key in keys:
                yield events.Key(key.value, sequence if len(sequence) == 1 else None)
            return
        # If keys is a string, the intention is that it's a mapping to a
        # character, which should really be treated as the sequence for the
        # purposes of the next step...
        if isinstance(keys, str):
            sequence = keys
        # If the sequence is a single character, attempt to process it as a
        # key.

        if len(sequence) == 1:
            try:
                if not sequence.isalnum():
                    name = _character_to_key(sequence)
                else:
                    name = sequence

                name = KEY_NAME_REPLACEMENTS.get(name, name)
                if len(name) == 1 and alt:
                    if name.isupper():
                        name = f"shift+{name.lower()}"
                    name = f"alt+{name}"
                yield events.Key(name, sequence)
            except Exception:
                yield events.Key(sequence, sequence)

END STEP

        Args:
            text_str: String with groups of digits, separated by one or more colons.

        Returns:
            A list of characters.
        """
        if not text_str:
            return [None]
        characters: list[str | None] = [
            chr(int(part)) if part.isdecimal() else chr(1)
            for part in text_str.split(":")
        ]
        return characters

    @lru_cache(maxsize=1024)
    def _parse_extended_key(self, sequence: str) -> list[events.Key] | None:
        """Parse a Kitty sequence.

        Args:
            sequence: Input sequence

        Returns:
            Key event, or `None` of none could be parsed.
        """

        if (match := _re_extended_key.fullmatch(sequence)) is None:
            return None

        key_events: list[events.Key] = []

        codes, end = match.groups(default="")
        codepoint_str, modifiers_str, text_str, *_ = codes.split(";") + ["", "", ""]
        codepoint = int(codepoint_str or "1")
        modifiers = int(modifiers_str or "0")

        for text in self._parse_colon_codepoints(text_str):
            if not (key := FUNCTIONAL_KEYS.get(f"{codepoint}{end}", "")):
                key = _character_to_key(text if text else chr(codepoint))

            key_tokens: list[str] = []
            # The modifier is redundant on a modifier key
            if (
                modifiers
                and key not in MODIFIER_FUNCTIONAL_KEYS
                and text_str is not None
            ):
                modifier_bits = int(modifiers) - 1
                # Not convinced of the utility in reporting caps_lock and num_lock
                MODIFIERS = ("alt", "ctrl", "super", "hyper", "meta")
                # Ignore caps_lock and num_lock modifiers
                if modifier_bits & 1 and (text is None or text.isspace()):
                    key_tokens.append("shift")
                for bit, modifier in enumerate(MODIFIERS, 1):
                    if modifier == "alt" and text is not None:
                        continue
                    if modifier_bits & (1 << bit):
                        key_tokens.append(modifier)

            key_tokens.sort()
            if key is not None:
                key_tokens.append(key)
            key_events.append(
                events.Key(
                    "+".join(key_tokens),
                    text
                    or (None if modifiers else SPECIAL_KEY_TO_CHARACTER.get(key, None)),
                )
            )
        return key_events

=== keyboard protocol 120-150 ===
    "57450u": "right_super",
    "57451u": "right_hyper",
    "57452u": "right_meta",
    "57453u": "iso_level3_shift",
    "57454u": "iso_level5_shift",
}

    "left_shift",
    "left_control",
    "left_alt",
    "left_super",
    "left_hyper",
    "left_meta",
    "right_shift",
    "right_control",
    "right_alt",
    "right_super",
    "right_hyper",
    "right_meta",
    "iso_level3_shift",
    "iso_level5_shift",
}

     1	from __future__ import annotations

     7	from textual.dom import DOMNode
     8	from textual.errors import DuplicateKeyHandlers
     9	from textual.message_pump import MessagePump
    10	
    11	
    12	async def dispatch_key(node: DOMNode, event: events.Key) -> bool:
    13	    """Dispatch a key event to method.
    14	
    15	    This function will call the method named 'key_<event.key>' on a node if it exists.
    16	    Some keys have aliases. The first alias found will be invoked if it exists.
    17	    If multiple handlers exist that match the key, an exception is raised.
    18	
    19	    Args:
    20	        event: A key event.
    21	
    22	    Returns:
    23	        True if key was handled, otherwise False.
    24	
    25	    Raises:
    26	        DuplicateKeyHandlers: When there's more than 1 handler that could handle this key.
    27	    """
    28	
    29	    def get_key_handler(pump: MessagePump, key: str) -> Callable | None:
    30	        """Look for the public and private handler methods by name on self."""
    31	        return getattr(pump, f"key_{key}", None) or getattr(pump, f"_key_{key}", None)
    32	
    33	    handled = False
    34	    invoked_method = None
    35	    key_name = event.name
    36	    if not key_name:
    37	        return False
    38	
    39	    def _raise_duplicate_key_handlers_error(
    40	        key_name: str, first_handler: str, second_handler: str
    41	    ) -> None:
    42	        """Raise exception for case where user presses a key and there are multiple candidate key handler methods for it."""
    43	        raise DuplicateKeyHandlers(
    44	            f"Multiple handlers for key press {key_name!r}.\n"
    45	            f"We found both {first_handler!r} and {second_handler!r}, "
    46	            f"and didn't know which to call.\n"
    47	            f"Consider combining them into a single handler.",
    48	        )
    49	
    50	    try:
    51	        screen = node.screen
    52	    except Exception:
    53	        screen = None
    54	    for key_method_name in event.name_aliases:
    55	        if (key_method := get_key_handler(node, key_method_name)) is not None:
    56	            if invoked_method:
    57	                _raise_duplicate_key_handlers_error(
    58	                    key_name, invoked_method.__name__, key_method.__name__
    59	                )
    60	            # If key handlers return False, then they are not considered handled
    61	            # This allows key handlers to do some conditional logic
    62	
    63	            if screen is not None and not screen.is_active:
    64	                break
    65	            handled = (await invoke(key_method, event)) is not False
    66	            invoked_method = key_method
    67	
    68	    return handled

    async def _check_bindings(self, key: str, priority: bool = False) -> bool:
        """Handle a key press.

        This method is used internally by the bindings system.

        Args:
            key: A key.
            priority: If `True` check from `App` down, otherwise from focused up.

        Returns:
            True if the key was handled by a binding, otherwise False
        """
        for namespace, bindings in (
            reversed(self.screen._binding_chain)
            if priority
            else self.screen._modal_binding_chain
        ):
            key_bindings = bindings.key_to_bindings.get(key, ())
            for binding in key_bindings:
                if binding.priority == priority:
                    if await self.run_action(binding.action, namespace):

import re
_re_extended_key = re.compile(
    r"\x1b\[(?P<number>\d+)(?:;(?P<modifiers>\d+))?(?:;(?P<text>\d+(?::\d+)*))?(?::(?P<event_type>[1-9]))?(?P<end>[u~ABCDEFHPQRS])"
)
cases = [
    "\x1b[97u", "\x1b[97;1u", "\x1b[97;2u", "\x1b[65;2u", "\x1b[97;3u",
    "\x1b[97;3:2u", "\x1b[97;3:3u", "\x1b[97;1;65u", "\x1b[97;1;65:2u",
    "\x1b[0;1;65u", "\x1b[61;2u", "\x1b[61;5u", "\x1b[49;6u",
    "\x1b[97;1;65:97u", "\x1b[97;1;65:2:1u", "\x1b[13~", "\x1b[1;2A",
    "\x1b[97;1;2u", "\x1b[97;1;2:2u", "\x1b[97;1;12u", "\x1b[97;1;12:3u",
]
for seq in cases:
    m = _re_extended_key.fullmatch(seq)
    print(repr(seq), "->", None if m is None else m.groupdict())

# Alternate key names for keys which produce a different character when shifted.
# The keys of this mapping are the Textual names of the shifted characters and
# the values are the Textual names of the unshifted (base) keys. For example,
# on a US layout pressing Shift+= produces "+", so "plus" is the shifted form
# of "equals_sign".
SHIFTED_KEY_NAMES: Final[dict[str, str]] = {
    "exclamation_mark": "1",
    "at": "2",
    "number_sign": "3",
    "dollar_sign": "4",
    "percent_sign": "5",

    "question_mark": "slash",
    "tilde": "grave_accent",

    """Return all aliases for the given key, including the key itself."""
    aliases = [key] + list(KEY_ALIASES.get(key, []))
    if "+" in key:
        modifiers, key_name = key.rpartition("+")[::2]
        base_key = SHIFTED_KEY_NAMES.get(key_name)
        if base_key is not None:
            # A shifted key (e.g. "plus") implies the shift modifier was held.
            # Add an alias using the unshifted base key so handlers declared with
            # either the shifted or unshifted form both match.
            if "shift" not in modifiers.split("+"):

new = '''def _get_key_aliases(key: str) -> list[str]:
    """Return all aliases for the given key, including the key itself."""
    aliases = [key] + list(KEY_ALIASES.get(key, []))
    if "+" in key:
        modifiers, key_name = key.rpartition("+")[::2]
    else:
        modifiers, key_name = "", key
    base_key = SHIFTED_KEY_NAMES.get(key_name)
    if base_key is not None and "shift" not in modifiers.split("+"):
        # A shifted key (e.g. "plus") implies the shift modifier was held.
        # Add an alias using the unshifted base key so handlers declared with
        # either the shifted or unshifted form both match.
        if modifiers:
            aliases.append(f"{modifiers}+shift+{base_key}")
        else:
            aliases.append(f"shift+{base_key}")

"""

Builtin events sent by Textual.

Events may be marked as "Bubbles" and "Verbose".
See the [events guide](/guide/events/#bubbling) for an explanation of bubbling.
Verbose events are excluded from the textual console, unless you explicitly request them with the `-v` switch as follows:

```
textual console -v
```
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Type, TypeVar

import rich.repr
from rich.style import Style
from typing_extensions import Self

from textual._types import CallbackType
from textual.geometry import Offset, Size
from textual.keys import _get_key_aliases
from textual.message import Message

MouseEventT = TypeVar("MouseEventT", bound="MouseEvent")

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from textual.dom import DOMNode
    from textual.timer import Timer as TimerClass
    from textual.timer import TimerCallback
    from textual.widget import Widget


@rich.repr.auto
class Event(Message):
    """The base class for all events."""

COMMAND:

path = "src/textual/events.py"
text = open(path).read()

old = '''    __slots__ = ["key", "character", "aliases"]

    def __init__(self, key: str, character: str | None) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.key = key
        """The key that was pressed."""
        self.character = (
            (key if len(key) == 1 else None) if character is None else character
        )
        """A printable character or ``None`` if it is not printable."""
        self.aliases: list[str] = _get_key_aliases(key)
        """The aliases for the key, including the key itself."""

    def __rich_repr__(self) -> rich.repr.Result:
        yield "key", self.key
        yield "character", self.character
        yield "name", self.name
        yield "is_printable", self.is_printable
        yield "aliases", self.aliases, [self.key]

    @property
    def name(self) -> str:
        """Name of a key suitable for use as a Python identifier."""
        return _key_to_identifier(self.key).lower()

    @property
    def name_aliases(self) -> list[str]:
        """The corresponding name for every alias in `aliases` list."""
        return [_key_to_identifier(key) for key in self.aliases]

    @property
    def is_printable(self) -> bool:
        """Check if the key is printable (produces a unicode character).

        Returns:
            `True` if the key is printable.
        """
        return False if self.character is None else self.character.isprintable()
'''

new = '''    __slots__ = [
        "key",
        "character",
        "aliases",
        "phase",
        "modifiers",
        "base_key",
        "shifted_key",
        "base_layout_key",
    ]

    def __init__(
        self,
        key: str,
        character: str | None,
        *,
        phase: str = "press",
        modifiers: Iterable[str] = (),
        base_key: str | None = None,
        shifted_key: str | None = None,
        base_layout_key: str | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        self.key = key
        """The key that was pressed."""
        self.character = (
            (key if len(key) == 1 else None) if character is None else character
        )
        """A printable character or ``None`` if it is not printable."""
        self.aliases: list[str] = _get_key_aliases(key)
        """The aliases for the key, including the key itself."""
        self.phase = phase
        """The phase of the key event: ``"press"``, ``"repeat"`` or ``"release"``."""
        self.modifiers: tuple[str, ...] = tuple(sorted(modifiers))
        """The modifiers held when the key was pressed, sorted alphabetically."""
        self.base_key: str = base_key if base_key is not None else key
        """The key in its unshifted (base) form."""
        self.shifted_key: str = shifted_key if shifted_key is not None else key
        """The key in its shifted form."""
        self.base_layout_key: str = (
            base_layout_key if base_layout_key is not None else key
        )
        """The key in the base layout (i.e. independent of the active keyboard layout)."""

    def __rich_repr__(self) -> rich.repr.Result:
        yield "key", self.key
        yield "character", self.character
        yield "name", self.name
        yield "is_printable", self.is_printable
        yield "phase", self.phase
        yield "modifiers", self.modifiers, ()
        yield "base_key", self.base_key, self.key
        yield "shifted_key", self.shifted_key, self.key
        yield "base_layout_key", self.base_layout_key, self.key
        yield "aliases", self.aliases, [self.key]

    def copy(self) -> Key:
        """Get a copy of this key event."""
        return Key(
            self.key,
            self.character,
            phase=self.phase,
            modifiers=self.modifiers,
            base_key=self.base_key,
            shifted_key=self.shifted_key,
            base_layout_key=self.base_layout_key,
        )

    @property
    def name(self) -> str:
        """Name of a key suitable for use as a Python identifier."""
        return _key_to_identifier(self.key).lower()

    @property
    def name_aliases(self) -> list[str]:
        """The corresponding name for every alias in `aliases` list."""
        return [_key_to_identifier(key) for key in self.aliases]

    @property
    def is_printable(self) -> bool:
        """Check if the key is printable (produces a unicode character).

        Returns:
            `True` if the key is printable.
        """
        return False if self.character is None else self.character.isprintable()

    @property
    def is_press(self) -> bool:
        """`True` if this is a key press event."""
        return self.phase == "press"

    @property
    def is_repeat(self) -> bool:
        """`True` if this is a key repeat event."""
        return self.phase == "repeat"

    @property
    def is_release(self) -> bool:
        """`True` if this is a key release event."""
        return self.phase == "release"

    @property
    def shift(self) -> bool:
        """`True` if the shift modifier was held."""
        return "shift" in self.modifiers

    @property
    def alt(self) -> bool:
        """`True` if the alt modifier was held."""
        return "alt" in self.modifiers

    @property
    def ctrl(self) -> bool:
        """`True` if the ctrl modifier was held."""
        return "ctrl" in self.modifiers

    @property
    def super(self) -> bool:
        """`True` if the super modifier was held."""
        return "super" in self.modifiers

    @property
    def hyper(self) -> bool:
        """`True` if the hyper modifier was held."""
        return "hyper" in self.modifiers

    @property
    def meta(self) -> bool:
        """`True` if the meta modifier was held."""
        return "meta" in self.modifiers
'''
assert old in text
text = text.replace(old, new, 1)

    23	    "^" + re.escape("\x1b[") + r"\?(?P<mode_id>\d+);(?P<setting_parameter>\d)\$y"
    24	)
    25	
    26	_re_cursor_position = re.compile(r"\x1b\[(?P<row>\d+);(?P<col>\d+)R")
    27	
    28	BRACKETED_PASTE_START: Final[str] = "\x1b[200~"
    29	"""Sequence received when a bracketed paste event starts."""
    30	BRACKETED_PASTE_END: Final[str] = "\x1b[201~"
    31	"""Sequence received when a bracketed paste event ends."""
    32	FOCUSIN: Final[str] = "\x1b[I"
    33	"""Sequence received when the terminal receives focus."""
    34	FOCUSOUT: Final[str] = "\x1b[O"
    35	"""Sequence received when focus is lost from the terminal."""
    36	
    37	SPECIAL_SEQUENCES = {BRACKETED_PASTE_START, BRACKETED_PASTE_END, FOCUSIN, FOCUSOUT}
    38	"""Set of special sequences."""
    39	
    40	_re_extended_key: Final = re.compile(r"\x1b\[(?:(\d+)(?:;(\d+))?)?([u~ABCDEFHPQRS])")

    42	    r"\x1b\[48;(\d+(?:\:.*?)?);(\d+(?:\:.*?)?);(\d+(?:\:.*?)?);(\d+(?:\:.*?)?)t"
    43	)
    44	
    45	
---
   325	            self._debug_log_file = None
   326	
   327	    def _sequence_to_key_events(
   328	        self, sequence: str, alt: bool = False
   329	    ) -> Iterable[events.Key]:
   330	        """Map a sequence of code points on to a sequence of keys.
   331	
   332	        Args:
   333	            sequence: Sequence of code points.
   334	
   335	        Returns:
   336	            Keys
   337	        """
   338	
   339	        if (match := _re_extended_key.fullmatch(sequence)) is not None:
   340	            number, modifiers, end = match.groups()
   341	            number = number or 1
   342	            if not (key := FUNCTIONAL_KEYS.get(f"{number}{end}", "")):
   343	                try:
   344	                    key = _character_to_key(chr(int(number)))
   345	                except Exception:
   346	                    key = chr(int(number))
   347	            key_tokens: list[str] = []
   348	            if modifiers:
   349	                modifier_bits = int(modifiers) - 1
   350	                # Not convinced of the utility in reporting caps_lock and num_lock
   351	                MODIFIERS = ("shift", "alt", "ctrl", "super", "hyper", "meta")
   352	                # Ignore caps_lock and num_lock modifiers
   353	                for bit, modifier in enumerate(MODIFIERS):
   354	                    if modifier_bits & (1 << bit):
   355	                        key_tokens.append(modifier)
   356	
   357	            key_tokens.sort()
   358	            key_tokens.append(key.lower())
   359	            yield events.Key(
   360	                "+".join(key_tokens), sequence if len(sequence) == 1 else None
   361	            )
   362	            return
   363	
   364	        keys = ANSI_SEQUENCES_KEYS.get(sequence)
   365	        # If we're being asked to ignore the key...
   366	        if keys is IGNORE_SEQUENCE:
   367	            # ...build a special ignore key event, which has the ignore
   368	            # name as the key (that is, the key this sequence is bound
   369	            # to is the ignore key) and the sequence that was ignored as
   370	            # the character.
   371	            yield events.Key(Keys.Ignore, sequence)
   372	            return
   373	        if isinstance(keys, tuple):
   374	            # If the sequence mapped to a tuple, then it's values from the
   375	            # `Keys` enum. Raise key events from what we find in the tuple.
   376	            for key in keys:
   377	                yield events.Key(key.value, sequence if len(sequence) == 1 else None)
   378	            return
   379	        # If keys is a string, the intention is that it's a mapping to a
   380	        # character, which should really be treated as the sequence for the
   381	        # purposes of the next step...
   382	        if isinstance(keys, str):
   383	            sequence = keys
   384	        # If the sequence is a single character, attempt to process it as a
   385	        # key.
   386	        if len(sequence) == 1:
   387	            try:
   388	                if not sequence.isalnum():
   389	                    name = _character_to_key(sequence)
   390	                else:
   391	                    name = sequence
   392	
   393	                name = KEY_NAME_REPLACEMENTS.get(name, name)
   394	                if len(name) == 1 and alt:
   395	                    if name.isupper():
   396	                        name = f"shift+{name.lower()}"
   397	                    name = f"alt+{name}"
   398	                yield events.Key(name, sequence)
   399	            except Exception:
   400	                yield events.Key(sequence, sequence)

END STEP

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import re
from functools import lru_cache
from typing import Any, Generator, Iterable

from typing_extensions import Final

from textual import constants, events, messages
from textual._ansi_sequences import ANSI_SEQUENCES_KEYS, IGNORE_SEQUENCE
from textual._keyboard_protocol import FUNCTIONAL_KEYS, MODIFIER_FUNCTIONAL_KEYS
from textual._parser import ParseEOF, Parser, ParseTimeout, Peek1, Read1, TokenCallback
from textual.keys import BASE_TO_SHIFTED_KEYS, KEY_NAME_REPLACEMENTS, Keys, _character_to_key
from textual.message import Message

# When trying to determine whether the current sequence is a supported/valid
# escape sequence, at which length should we give up and consider our search
# to be unsuccessful?
_MAX_SEQUENCE_SEARCH_THRESHOLD = 32

_re_mouse_event = re.compile("^" + re.escape("\x1b[") + r"(<?[-\d;]+[mM]|M...)\Z")
_re_terminal_mode_response = re.compile(
    "^" + re.escape("\x1b[") + r"\?(?P<mode_id>\d+);(?P<setting_parameter>\d)\$y"
)

_re_cursor_position = re.compile(r"\x1b\[(?P<row>\d+);(?P<col>\d+)R")

BRACKETED_PASTE_START: Final[str] = "\x1b[200~"
"""Sequence received when a bracketed paste event starts."""
BRACKETED_PASTE_END: Final[str] = "\x1b[201~"
"""Sequence received when a bracketed paste event ends."""
FOCUSIN: Final[str] = "\x1b[I"
"""Sequence received when the terminal receives focus."""
FOCUSOUT: Final[str] = "\x1b[O"
"""Sequence received when focus is lost from the terminal."""

SPECIAL_SEQUENCES = {BRACKETED_PASTE_START, BRACKETED_PASTE_END, FOCUSIN, FOCUSOUT}
"""Set of special sequences."""


SPECIAL_KEY_TO_CHARACTER: Final = {
    "backspace": "\x7f",
    "enter": "\r",
    "tab": "\t",
}
"""Explicit characters for special keys, used in Kitty protocol parsing."""

# Characters produced when a US-layout key is pressed together with Shift.
_SHIFTED_CHARACTERS: Final = {
    "1": "!",
    "2": "@",
    "3": "#",
    "4": "$",
    "5": "%",
    "6": "^",
    "7": "&",
    "8": "*",

    "0": ")",

    "=": "+",

    "]": "}",

    "'": '"',
    ",": "<",
    ".": ">",
    "/": "?",
    "`": "~",
}

_MODIFIER_NAMES: Final = ("shift", "alt", "ctrl", "super", "hyper", "meta")

cd /app && python - <<'PY'
path = "src/textual/_xterm_parser.py"
text = open(path).read()
marker = "    def _sequence_to_key_events("
idx = text.index(marker)
text = text[:idx] + r'''    @staticmethod
    def _modifiers_from_bits(modifier_bits: int) -> tuple[str, ...]:
        """Convert a Kitty modifier bitmask to a sorted tuple of modifier names."""
        return tuple(
            sorted(
                name
                for bit, name in enumerate(_MODIFIER_NAMES)
                if modifier_bits & (1 << bit)
            )
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _parse_colon_codepoints(text_str: str | None) -> list[str | None]:
        """Convert codepoints split on colons in to a list of characters.

        Args:
            text_str: String with groups of digits, separated by colons.

        Returns:
            A list of characters (or `[None]` if no text was supplied).
        """
        if not text_str:
            return [None]
        return [
            chr(int(part)) if part.isdecimal() else chr(1)
            for part in text_str.split(":")
        ]

    @staticmethod
    def _event_from_key_name(
        name: str, character: str | None, alt: bool = False
    ) -> events.Key:
        """Build a key event from a Textual key name.

        Args:
            name: A key name, e.g. ``"enter"`` or ``"ctrl+a"``.
            character: The character produced by the key, if any.
            alt: If `True`, apply an ``alt`` prefix to the key.

        Returns:
            A `Key` event with stable metadata.
        """
        parts = name.split("+")
        modifiers = tuple(sorted(parts[:-1])) if len(parts) > 1 else ()
        base_key = parts[-1]
        shifted_key = base_key
        if len(base_key) == 1 and base_key.isupper() and (alt or "shift" in modifiers):
            # An upper-case letter implies shift was held. Normalise the base
            # key to its lower-case form.
            base_key = base_key.lower()
            if "shift" not in modifiers:
                modifiers = tuple(sorted((*modifiers, "shift")))
            shifted_key = base_key.upper()
        if alt:
            modifiers = tuple(sorted((*modifiers, "alt")))
        key = "+".join((*modifiers, base_key))
        return events.Key(
            key,
            character,
            modifiers=modifiers,
            base_key=base_key,
            shifted_key=shifted_key,
            base_layout_key=base_key,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _kitty_key_event(
        *,
        number: int,
        end: str,
        modifiers: tuple[str, ...],
        text: str | None,
        phase: str,
        functional_key: str,
    ) -> events.Key:
        """Create a `Key` event from parsed Kitty keyboard protocol fields.

        Args:
            number: The key code.
            end: The terminating character of the sequence.
            modifiers: The sorted tuple of modifier names.
            text: The text produced by the key, if reported.
            phase: ``"press"``, ``"repeat"`` or ``"release"``.
            functional_key: The Textual key name for a functional key, or empty.

        Returns:
            A `Key` event with stable metadata.
        """
        if functional_key:
            # A functional / special key (arrows, f-keys, modifier keys, ...).
            base_key = shifted_key = base_layout_key = functional_key
            if functional_key in MODIFIER_FUNCTIONAL_KEYS:
                public_key = functional_key
                public_modifiers: tuple[str, ...] = ()
            else:
                public_modifiers = modifiers
                public_key = "+".join((*sorted(public_modifiers), functional_key))
            if text is not None:
                character = text
            elif not modifiers:
                character = SPECIAL_KEY_TO_CHARACTER.get(functional_key)
            else:
                character = None
            return events.Key(
                public_key,
                character,
                phase=phase,
                modifiers=modifiers,
                base_key=base_key,
                shifted_key=shifted_key,
                base_layout_key=base_layout_key,
            )

        if number == 0:
            # Associated-text-only key: use the text as both the key and character.
            key_name = (
                _character_to_key(text) if text is not None and len(text) == 1 else ""
            )
            if not key_name:
                key_name = text if text is not None else ""
            public_key = (
                "+".join((*sorted(modifiers), key_name)) if modifiers else key_name
            )
            return events.Key(
                public_key,
                text,
                phase=phase,
                modifiers=modifiers,
                base_key=key_name,
                shifted_key=key_name,
                base_layout_key=key_name,
            )

        base_character = chr(number)
        if "shift" in modifiers:
            # Normalise an alternate key code (e.g. 'A' or '+') back to the
            # unshifted base key.
            if base_character.isupper():
                base_character = base_character.lower()
            else:
                for base_char, shifted_char in _SHIFTED_CHARACTERS.items():
                    if base_character == shifted_char:
                        base_character = base_char
                        break
        base_key = _character_to_key(base_character)
        base_layout_key = base_key

        if text is not None:
            shifted_character = text
        elif "shift" in modifiers:
            if base_character.isalpha():
                shifted_character = base_character.upper()
            else:
                shifted_character = _SHIFTED_CHARACTERS.get(
                    base_character, base_character
                )
        else:
            shifted_character = base_character

        shifted_key = (
            _character_to_key(shifted_character)
            if len(shifted_character) == 1
            else shifted_character
        )

        non_shift_modifiers = tuple(
            modifier for modifier in modifiers if modifier != "shift"
        )
        if text is not None:
            character = text if not non_shift_modifiers else None
        else:
            character = shifted_character if not non_shift_modifiers else None

        # Alternate keys (e.g. Shift+= produces "+") are referred to by their
        # shifted name in the public key, with the implicit shift dropped.
        alternate_key = BASE_TO_SHIFTED_KEYS.get(base_key)
        if alternate_key is not None and shifted_key == alternate_key:
            public_modifiers = non_shift_modifiers
            public_key_name = shifted_key
        else:
            public_modifiers = modifiers
            public_key_name = base_key

        public_key = "+".join((*sorted(public_modifiers), public_key_name))
        return events.Key(
            public_key,
            character,
            phase=phase,
            modifiers=modifiers,
            base_key=base_key,
            shifted_key=shifted_key,
            base_layout_key=base_layout_key,
        )

    @classmethod
    @lru_cache(maxsize=1024)
    def _parse_extended_key(cls, sequence: str) -> list[events.Key] | None:
        """Parse a Kitty keyboard protocol sequence.

        Args:
            sequence: The input sequence.

        Returns:
            A list of key events, or `None` if the sequence could not be parsed.
        """
        if (match := _re_extended_key.fullmatch(sequence)) is None:
            return None

        number = int(match["number"])
        modifier_bits = int(match["modifiers"] or 1) - 1
        event_type = int(match["event_type"] or 1)
        text_str = match["text"]
        end = match["end"]

        phase = _PHASES.get(event_type, "press")
        modifiers = cls._modifiers_from_bits(modifier_bits)
        functional_key = FUNCTIONAL_KEYS.get(f"{number}{end}", "")

        return [
            cls._kitty_key_event(
                number=number,
                end=end,
                modifiers=modifiers,
                text=text,
                phase=phase,
                functional_key=functional_key,
            )
            for text in cls._parse_colon_codepoints(text_str)
        ]

    def _sequence_to_key_events(
        self, sequence: str, alt: bool = False
    ) -> Iterable[events.Key]:
        """Map a sequence of code points on to a sequence of keys.

        Args:
            sequence: Sequence of code points.
            alt: Whether an alt prefix should be applied to the resulting keys.

        Returns:
            Keys
        """

        if (key_events := self._parse_extended_key(sequence)) is not None:
            for event in key_events:
                yield event.copy()
            return

        keys = ANSI_SEQUENCES_KEYS.get(sequence)
        # If we're being asked to ignore the key...
        if keys is IGNORE_SEQUENCE:
            # ...build a special ignore key event, which has the ignore
            # name as the key (that is, the key this sequence is bound
            # to is the ignore key) and the sequence that was ignored as
            # the character.
            yield events.Key(Keys.Ignore, sequence)
            return
        if isinstance(keys, tuple):
            # If the sequence mapped to a tuple, then it's values from the
            # `Keys` enum. Raise key events from what we find in the tuple.
            for key in keys:
                yield self._event_from_key_name(
                    key.value, sequence if len(sequence) == 1 else None, alt=alt
                )
            return
        # If keys is a string, the intention is that it's a mapping to a
        # character, which should really be treated as the sequence for the
        # purposes of the next step...
        if isinstance(keys, str):
            sequence = keys
        # If the sequence is a single character, attempt to process it as a
        # key.
        if len(sequence) == 1:
            try:
                if not sequence.isalnum():
                    name = _character_to_key(sequence)
                else:
                    name = sequence

                name = KEY_NAME_REPLACEMENTS.get(name, name)
                yield self._event_from_key_name(name, sequence, alt=alt)
            except Exception:
                yield self._event_from_key_name(sequence, sequence, alt=alt)
'''
open(path, "w").write(text)
print("written")

def parse(seq):
    p = XTermParser()
    evs = list(p.feed(seq))
    try:
        evs.extend(p.feed(""))
    except Exception:
        pass
    return evs

from textual.app import App, ComposeResult
from textual.containers import Grid
from textual.widgets import Footer, Markdown, Placeholder

HELP = """\
## Breakpoints

A demonstration of how to make an app respond to the dimensions of the terminal.

Try resizing the terminal, then have a look at the source to see how it works!
"""


class BreakpointApp(App):

    # A breakpoint consists of a width and a class name to set
    HORIZONTAL_BREAKPOINTS = [
        (0, "-narrow"),
        (40, "-normal"),
        (80, "-wide"),
        (120, "-very-wide"),
    ]

    CSS = """
    Screen {        
        Placeholder { padding: 2; }
        Grid { grid-rows: auto; height: auto; }
        # Change the styles according to the breakpoint classes
        &.-narrow {
            Grid { grid-size: 1; }
        }
        &.-normal {
            Grid { grid-size: 2; }
        }
        &.-wide {
            Grid { grid-size: 4; }
        }
        &.-very-wide {
            Grid { grid-size: 6; }
        }

"""
An implementation of a classic calculator, with a layout inspired by macOS calculator.

Works like a real calculator. Click the buttons or press the equivalent keys.
"""

from decimal import Decimal

from textual import events, on
from textual.app import App, ComposeResult
from textual.containers import Container
from textual.css.query import NoMatches
from textual.reactive import var
from textual.widgets import Button, Digits


class CalculatorApp(App):
    """A working 'desktop' calculator."""

    CSS_PATH = "calculator.tcss"

    numbers = var("0")
    show_ac = var(True)
    left = var(Decimal("0"))
    right = var(Decimal("0"))
    value = var("")
    operator = var("plus")

    # Maps button IDs on to the corresponding key name
    NAME_MAP = {
        "asterisk": "multiply",
        "slash": "divide",
        "underscore": "plus-minus",
        "full_stop": "point",
        "plus_minus_sign": "plus-minus",
        "percent_sign": "percent",
        "equals_sign": "equals",
